       compute q/max/soft/grad

Details:
- Precompute L.
- `soft_and_grad(p, beta)`:
```
m=2*n-1
pad = np.zeros(L); pad[:n]=p
P = np.fft.rfft(pad)
Qfull = np.fft.irfft(P*P, n=L)
q = Qfull[:m]
# numerically clip small negatives
q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
z = beta*q
z -= z.max()
ez = np.exp(z)
Z = ez.sum()
s = ez/Z
# score soft or max
maxq = q.max()
softL = (z.max() + np.log(Z))/beta
s_pad = np.zeros(L); s_pad[:m]=s
# p_rev
p_rev = np.zeros(L); p_rev[0]=p[0]
if n>1: p_rev[L-n+1:L] = p[n-1:0:-1]
Sp = np.fft.rfft(s_pad)
Pr = np.fft.rfft(p_rev)
gp = 2.0*np.fft.irfft(Sp*Pr, n=L)[:n]
# grad theta
grad = gp - p*np.sum(gp)
return p, q, maxq, softL, grad
```
But computing `Pr` every iter is expensive. We can compute `Fp` from P? `Pr = fft(p_rev)`. Is there relation to P? For real p, F(p_rev) = Fp^{-1}? Specifically DFT of reversed indices: P_rev[k] = P[-k] = P[L-k]. In compact rfft, for k=0.., Fp_rev[k] = conjugate? Since p_rev_i = p_{-i mod L}, F(p_rev)[k] = Σ p_{-i} e^{2πik i/L}=Σ p_j e^{-2πik j/L}=conj(P[k]). For real P? Wait P[k]=Σ p_j e^{-2πikj/L}; reverse gives Σ p_j e^{+2πikj/L}=conj(P[k]) for real p. Let's test p=[a,b], P; rfft compact. Fp_rev[0]=P0, for k>0 = conj(P[k]). But p_rev has p_k at index L-k, yes. So we don't need construct p_rev each iter! `Pr_compact = np.conjugate(P)`? Careful rfft returns P[0..L/2], and reverse DFT compact: for k > L/2 not represented; but conj(P[k]) works for compact? Since F(reverse)_k = conj(Fp_k) for all k. For real p. Let's verify with p=[a,b,0,0], L=4. P = [a+b, a-b, a+b? rfft length3: P0=a+b, P1=(a+ i? wait DFT k=1: a + b e^{-iπ/2}=a - i b), P2=a+b]. Reverse p_rev=[a,0,0,b]; F_rev k=1: a + b e^{-iπ/3? L=4 e^{-iπ/2} b*? p_rev at index3 b e^{-i3π/2}=b i? Actually DFT F[l]=Σ x_i e^{-2πi l i/L}. l=1: x0 a + x3 b e^{-i3π/2}=a + i b. conj(P1)=a + i b. yes. For l=2: P2=a+b, conj same. So yes Fp_rev = conj(P). Great. Then gradient = 2 irfft(fft(s_pad) * conj(P)). Even better. But wait `P` is compact rfft of padded p. We already computed it. So no p_rev.

Test n=2, s at q1. P compact length3 [a+b, a-ib, a+b]. Sp? s_pad [0,s,0,0]. iff. Product. Inverse should give? Let's trust.

Thus gradient function efficient: compute P once, Q, s_pad, Sp= rfft(s_pad), gp = 2*irfft(Sp*np.conj(P), n=L)[:n]. Need ensure derivative for linear conv p*p: Q = ifft(P^2). Gradient wrt p is 2 ifft(F(s) * conj(P))? Let's derive with Fourier: q=I(P^2). dq =2I(P dP). dP=F(dp). s·dq =2 Re? s real pad. In frequency, s·I(U) = F(s)_0? Actually inner product time = (1/L) Σ F(s)^* F(U). Since s real, F(s)^*=F(s) for Hermitian? Not if s not symmetric. But gp_i = 2 Σ_k s_k p_{k-i}. Fourier F(gp) = 2 F(s) *? The cross-correlation gp_i = 2 (s *? p)_i = 2 Σ_k s_k p_{k-i}. DFT of gp: Σ_i 2Σ_k s_k p_{k-i} e^{-iω_i}. Let j=k-i => i=k-j; exponent e^{-iω(k-j)} = e^{-iωk} e^{iωj}. Sum j p_j e^{iωj} = conj(P[ω]); sum k s_k e^{-iωk}=F(s). So F(gp)=2 F(s) conj(P). Good. If s real? F(s) not conjugate but product as complex. Inverse yields real. Use `np.fft.irfft(Sp * np.conjugate(P), n=L)`. Good.

So we only need P for Q and gradient. Good.

Now optimization loop:
```
def optimize_from_p_init(p0, n, L, time_budget, beta_start, beta_end, lr_start, lr_end, ...):
    theta = np.log(p0 + tiny)
    theta -= theta.max()
    Adam state m,v
    best_score = 2*n*maxq from self
    best_p = p0
    for it in range(max):
       if time exceeded break
       p = softmax(theta)
       # maybe enforce p minimum and renormalize (softmax does)
       P = rfft(pad)
       Q=irfft(P*P)
       q=Q[:m]
       maxq = max(q)
       score=2*n*maxq
       if score < best_score*(1-1e-6): update best
       # weights
       # use temperature based on current max and beta
       # Maybe use active set: s = exp((q - maxq)/temp) / sum
       temp = max(1e-12, maxq / beta?)  # Equivalent beta=1/temp? z=beta(q) with qmax; weights = exp(beta(q-qmax))/sum. beta anneal.
       s = exp(beta*(q - maxq)); sum.
       s_pad[:m]=s
       Sp = rfft(s_pad)
       gp=2*irfft(Sp*conj(P),L)[:n]
       grad=gp - p*np.sum(gp)
       # Adam update
       m = ...; v=...
       theta -= step * mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps)
       theta -= theta.mean()
       # maybe periodically renormalize theta to avoid overflow: if max abs > 50, scale?
       # beta anneal: beta = beta_start * (beta_end/beta_start)**(it/maxit) or sqrt
```

Let's think of potential pitfalls in gradient optimizer:
- Softmax θ may underflow if values differ >700. Initialize θ from log p with p maybe zeros? Use p initialized positive by adding tiny. Keep θ centered; if range >50, scale θ by 0.5 or clip. But scaling θ changes p substantially; we can renormalize θ to range [-20,20] while preserving relative? Could clip p to min 1e-20 then log. Simpler: maintain p directly and use multiplicative updates without log overflow.
- Direct multiplicative update: p_new = p * exp(η * A) / sum, where A from gradient direction. This avoids θ. Since p>0, log p = log. Update log p by step A. We can just update p multiplicatively:
   for gradient descent: p ← p * exp(-η * grad_p_normalized?) But with normalization, if we do p = p * exp(-η*(gp - p*sumgp?))? The log update: θ_i += η * (p_i - p_shift_i) *? That corresponds p_i multiply by exp(η(...)). Since θ=log p, adding logarithmic increment. We can maintain p and apply p *= exp(step) then normalize. If step large, overflow; clip step to [-1,1]. This is equivalent and simpler.

Let's derive multiplicative update from soft weights:
We want minimize soft objective. In log parameters, descent direction D = -gradθ = - (gp - p s) = p s - gp. Take small η: p_new ∝ p * exp(η (p s - gp)). Since gp=2 cross correlation weights. For inside, gp_i=2Σs_k p_{k-i}. s sum maybe not 1? We'll normalize s sum=1; gp sum=2r (boundary effect). Then p s - gp. For η small. To keep stable, clip log_update to [-λ, λ]/? Scale: gp values ~1/n, p~1/n, difference ~1/n. If η=1, log_update O(1/n) ~0.0002, too slow. We need multiply by n perhaps. Since we only care direction, we can use `D = p * sum_gp - gp` (ord/n) and then normalize by typical magnitude. Let scale = max(np.max(np.abs(D))) maybe ~1/n. Use `step = lr * D / scale`, clip to [-0.5,0.5]. Then p *= exp(step); normalize. This gives multiplicative factor up to exp(0.5)=1.65 per iter; okay.

This is like mirror descent. It may be stable. Adam on θ may be equivalent but easier to adapt step magnitude using normalized D.

Let's implement `multiplicative_step(p, s, P, L, n, lr)`:
- Compute gp (2*irfft(...)).
- D = p*sum(gp) - gp  (descent direction for log p) (since p*sumg - gp = - (gp - p sumg)). This vector sum? Use D.
- Clip small negative p? p all positive.
- scale = max(np.ptp(D), 1e-12) maybe or max(abs(D)). Use normalize by max(abs(D)) to get range [-1,1]. Then p *= exp(step * clip(D, -1,1)?). If D has both signs with max ~1 after scaling, step around 0.1-0.5.
Actually if D normalized by max, some coords step=sign. We can use `u = D / np.max(np.abs(D))`; `delta = lr * u`; p = p*np.exp(delta); p /= sum.
But if D mostly small with one coord large, others no update; okay.
Learning rate lr can be 0.05 to 0.3.

This avoids θ/Adam. But using p directly can make small probabilities decrease/increase; okay.

Weights s: We can choose active weights as `s = np.exp((q - maxq)/temp) / sum`, with temp = maxq / β? Equivalent β=1/(maxq/temp?) Let's define `beta` dimensionless: z = q / temp, then zmax=1, weights=exp(q/temp -1). Use temp = maxq / beta? If beta=10, weights from 1 down to exp(-) for q much smaller. temp = maxq/beta. Then β = 1/temp? Actually if temp=maxq/beta, z=q/temp = beta q / maxq; max=beta. Good. So anneal beta from 5 to 200 maybe.
For sharp subgradient, beta large. For early, beta small to smooth.

We can also use topK active: set s = (q > maxq - delta) / count, with delta = maxq * eps. But softmax with beta easier.

`gp` computation requires FFT each iteration. Could we update P incrementally? Not necessary.

Optimization loop using multiplicative updates may stagnate in local min. We can add random restarts: at intervals, perturb p by multiplying random smooth noise (block) and anneal back. Also multiple initial shapes.

Let's detail `run_fft_optimize`:
Input p (sum1, n>0). Output best p and score_self.
Precompute L. 
Algorithm:
```
rng maybe.
best = p.copy(); best_score = 2*n*maxq(p)
it=0
lr = 0.2
beta = 10.0
while time < limit:
   p = np.clip(p, 1e-300, None); p/=sum
   P = rfft(p_pad)
   Qfull = irfft(P*P)
   q = Qfull[:m]; q = np.maximum(q,0)
   maxq = q.max()
   score=2*n*maxq
   if score < best_score*(1-1e-8): update best
   if time and exact? maybe callback.
   # weights: choose beta annealed over iterations. We'll track frac = it/max_it? Use time.
   # beta = beta_start + (beta_end-beta_start)*(tfrac)^0.5 maybe up to 200.
   temp = maxq / beta if maxq>0 else 1e-12
   z = q/temp
   z -= z.max()
   ez = np.exp(np.minimum(z, 700)) # zmax 1 if beta? Actually zmax=beta.
   s = ez/ez.sum()
   s_pad[:m]=s
   Sp = rfft(s_pad)
   gp=2*irfft(Sp*np.conjugate(P), L)[:n]
   # normalize D
   sum_gp = gp.sum()
   D = p*sum_gp - gp
   # optionally center D? Already log gradient mean? D sum = sum p sumg - sumg =? not zero. But log update adding constant doesn't affect normalized p; we can subtract mean of D to reduce drift? Constant shift in D doesn't matter after normalization? p *= exp(c + D_i) -> multiply all by e^c, normalization removes. So we can subtract D.mean() to zero mean.
   D -= D.mean()
   maxabs = np.max(np.abs(D))
   if maxabs > 1e-12:
       delta = lr * D / maxabs
       # clip to avoid huge
       np.clip(delta, -0.5, 0.5, out=delta)
       p = p * np.exp(delta)
       p /= p.sum()
   # occasionally random kick if stuck:
   if it % kick_period ==0:
       p *= exp(smooth noise)
       p /= p.sum()
   it++
   # anneal lr: lr = lr_max * (lr_min/lr_max)**(it/max_iter?) or based time; beta increase
```

Let's outline:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _valid_sequence(a): ...
def _normalize(a): ...
def _next_pow2(x): ...
def _fft_conv_info(p, L, n):
    m=2*n-1
    pad=np.zeros(L)
    pad[:n]=p
    P=np.fft.rfft(pad)
    Q=np.fft.irfft(P*P, n=L)
    q=Q[:m]
    # clip small negative
    q[np.abs(q)<1e-15]=0? Use np.maximum
    maxq=float(np.max(q))
    return P,q,maxq
```
Note: For p sum1, P0=1? Numpy FFT unnormalized forward, inverse 1/L. If p sum1, P[0]=1. Q values are convolution coefficients? Let's check scaling. `np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(x)*np.fft.rfft(y), n=L)` returns circular convolution of x,y (not normalized extra). For x=[a,b,0], y same, L=4, result [a^2,2ab,b^2,0]. Good. So q correct.

`_weights(q,maxq,beta)`:
```
if maxq <=0: return uniform? else:
temp=max(1e-300, maxq/beta)
z=q/temp - beta  # max=qmax/temp=beta -> zmax=0
# z <=0 maybe, but due roundoff can positive; subtract max
z2=z-z.max()
ez=np.exp(z2)
s=ez/ez.sum()
return s
```
Here q/temp max = beta, z2 max after subtract 0. Actually if set `z = q/temp`, max=beta. z2=z-beta. Good.
Beta large -> weights sharp.

`_gradient_from_weights(p, s, P, L, n)`:
```
s_pad=np.zeros(L); s_pad[:2*n-1]=s
Sp=np.fft.rfft(s_pad)
gp=2.0*np.fft.irfft(Sp*np.conjugate(P), n=L)
gp=gp[:n].astype(float)
return gp
```
Potential issue: `np.conjugate(P)` compact; for k=0 real. Good.

`_mul_update(p, gp, lr)`:
```
s_gp=gp.sum()
D=p*s_gp - gp
D -= D.mean()
maxabs=np.max(np.abs(D))
if maxabs<1e-15: return p
delta=lr*D/maxabs
if lr_clip: np.clip(delta, -1.0, 1.0, out=delta) # maybe 0.5
p=p*np.exp(delta)
p=p/p.sum()
return p
```
If gp has tiny negative due numerical, okay.

But is D correct for multiplicative log update? The descent direction for log p is -gradθ = p*s_gp - gp. We add `lr * D / max`. Yes.

What about learning rate scaling? If D is normalized to max1, delta max lr. If lr=0.1, factor 1.1. Number iterations many. Could be good. We can make lr anneal: start 0.3, end 0.05.

`run_full_opt`:
Inputs: p0, n, L, m, time_alloc, beta_start, beta_end, lr_start, lr_end, rng, record? We need possibly evaluate exact occasionally. We can pass a `require exact` conflict with no closures. We can inside use `evaluate_sequence` directly (assumed imported). But if `evaluate_sequence` not in scope? It is. We can call.

Need manage time: Before phase, `t_end=time.monotonic()+alloc`. At end of `propose_candidate`, final return by deadline.

In loop, exact evaluation if `score_self < best_exact + 0.01` and time since last exact >5s or every 1000 iters. But exact maybe slow; keep count.

Pseudo:
```
def run_full_opt(p0, n, L, m, budget, rng, do_exact=True, exact_period=10.0):
    t0=time.monotonic(); t_end=t0+max(1.0,budget)
    p=np.clip(p0,1e-300,None).copy(); p/=p.sum()
    P,q,maxq=_fft_conv_info...
    best=p.copy(); best_self=2*n*maxq
    best_exact=best_self*1e9? maybe from p0 exact if do_exact
    last_exact=t0
    it=0
    max_it= max(1000, int(budget*500)) # not needed use time
    while time.monotonic()<t_end and (do_exact? best_exact>target maybe):
        frac = min(1.0,(time.monotonic()-t0)/budget)
        beta = beta_start*(1 + (beta_end/beta_start-1)*frac)
        lr = lr_start + (lr_end-lr_start)*frac
        P,q,maxq=... # could reuse from previous? We compute fresh.
        score=2*n*maxq
        if score < best_self: ...
        if do_exact and (time.monotonic()-last_exact > exact_period) and (score < best_exact + 0.02 or it%5000==0):
            val=evaluate_sequence(p.tolist())
            if finite and val<best_exact: best_exact=val; best_exactseq=p.copy(); 
            last_exact=time.monotonic()
        s=_weights(q,maxq,beta)
        gp=_gradient_from_weights(p,s,P,L,n)
        p=_mul_update(p,gp,lr)
        # random kick every 5000 it if stuck
        if it % 3000 == 0 and it>0:
             noise = smooth noise small; p = p*exp(noise); normalize
        it+=1
    # final exact for best_self and best_exactseq
    if do_exact:
       v1=evaluate_sequence(best.tolist()); ...
       v2=evaluate_sequence(best_exactseq.tolist()) if exists
    return best_exact_seq, best_exact_score (or self best if no exact)
```

```
def block_to_full(m, B, n, starts, lengths, inv_len):
   p=np.empty(n)
   for j in range(B): p[starts[j]:starts[j+1]] = m[j]*inv_len[j]
   p/=p.sum()
```

Resampling function:
```
def resample_to_length(seq, n):
   a=np.asarray(seq,float)
   if a.size==0: return ones/n
   a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<=0: return ones/n
   a/=s
   if a.size==n: return a
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
   b=np.maximum(b,1e-300); b/=b.sum(); return b
```

def generate_inits(n, rng, include_height):
   inits=[]
   inits.append(np.ones(n)/n)
   # two-level both orientations
   p=np.empty(n); p[:n//2]=4/3; p[n//2:]=2/3; normalize
   inits.append(p); inits.append(p[::-1])
   # three-level maybe: high, mid, low. Use masses [0.5,0.3,0.2] etc. Could generate by block step:
   for ratios in [(2/3,1/3),(0.5,0.3,0.2),(0.4,0.3,0.3),(0.3,0.4,0.3)]: ... make plateau lengths n/B.
   # exponentials
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   for gamma in [-8,-5,-3,-2,2,3,5,8]: p=exp(gamma*t)
   # powers: (1-t+eps)^alpha, t^alpha with alpha -? nonnegative: alpha [0.2,0.5,1,2,4]
   # logit smooth: f=1+3*sigmoid((0.3-t)/0.1) etc
   # sinusoidal: 1+0.8*sin(2πt+phase) for phase random? Ensure positive.
   # random smooth: Gaussian mixture 2-3 terms.
```

Let's write `run_block_opt` carefully for time.

```
def run_block_opt(p0, n, L, B, budget, rng, full_exact=False):
    t_end=time.monotonic()+budget
    lengths=np.full(B, n//B, dtype=int); lengths[:n%B]+=1
    starts=np.concatenate(([0], np.cumsum(lengths)))
    rep=np.repeat(np.arange(B), lengths)
    inv_len=1.0/lengths
    m=_normalize(np.zeros? block sums p0)
    best_m=m.copy(); best_score=inf
    last_exact...
    it=0
    while time<t_end:
       p=m[rep]*inv_len[rep]; p/=p.sum()
       P,q,maxq=_fft_conv_info
       score=2*n*maxq
       if score<best_score: update best_m,p
       # maybe exact if ...
       beta = ...
       s=_weights
       gp=_grad
       block_sums=np.add.reduceat(gp, starts[:-1]) # for B
       grad_m=block_sums/lengths
       D=m*grad_m.sum() - grad_m; D-=D.mean()
       maxabs=...
       if maxabs>...:
          delta=lr*D/maxabs; clip; m=m*exp(delta); m.normalize
       it++
    # at end exact best_p
```
`np.add.reduceat` if B=1? starts[:-1] length1 [0], returns full sum. Good. Need ensure gp length n. If gp has tiny imaginary? irfft returns real.

`_weights` uses maxq; if q all zeros? p positive so no.

`_fft_conv_info` recomputes P; in block opt we need P for gradient; fine.

Functions:

import numpy as np

`_as_prob(a, n=None)`:
```
def _as_prob(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return None
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    np.clip(a, 0.0, None, out=a)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s): return None
    a = a/s
    a = np.clip(a, 1e-20, None)
    a = a/a.sum()
    return a
```
For resampling to n, insert minimum after.

`_resample(a, n)`:
```
a = _as_prob(a)
if a is None: return np.ones(n)/n
if a.size == n: return np.clip(a,1e-20,None); normalize
x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,a.size)
x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
b=_as_prob(b); if b is None: return ones
return b
```
Need ensure `_as_prob` returns new array; if b None.

`_next_pow2(x)`:
```
x=int(x); p=1
while p < x: p <<=1
return p
```

`_fft_conv(p, L, n)`:
```
m=2*n-1
pad=np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
pad[:n]=p
P=np.fft.rfft(pad)
Q=np.fft.irfft(P*P, n=L)
q=Q[:m]
# q could have tiny negatives; set to 0 if > -1e-12 maybe
q = np.where(q < 0.0, 0.0, q) # since nonnegative
maxq=float(np.max(q)) if m>0 else 0.0
return P,q,maxq
```
`np.where` allocate; okay.

`_score_from_max(maxq,n)=2*n*maxq`.

`_weights_from_q(q, maxq, beta, top_k=0)`:
```
if maxq <= 1e-300:
    s = np.full(q.size, 1.0/q.size)
    return s
temp = maxq / max(1.0, beta)
z = q/temp
z -= z.max()
# to avoid under/overflow: z max 0, min maybe -large; clip lower -745
z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 745.0)
ez=np.exp(z)
if top_k:
   # add small uniform to top K? Maybe use top_k in separate
s=ez/ez.sum()
if top_k and q.size>top_k:
   idx=np.argpartition(q, -top_k)[-top_k:]
   s[idx] += 2.0  # boost top? Normalize
   s/=s.sum()
return s
```
Adding 2 to top K gives significant but not exclusive. Or we can use in full opt. Might not need.

`_grad_from_p(s, P, L, n)`:
```
mp=2*n-1
sp=np.zeros(L)

`_smooth_noise(n, rng, amp)`:
```
noise=np.zeros(n)
nterm=int(rng.integers(1,4))
idx=np.arange(n)
for _ in range(nterm):
   center=float(rng.uniform(0,n))
   width=float(rng.uniform(n/50, n/8+1))
   scale=float(rng.normal(0,amp))
   noise += scale*np.exp(-0.5*((idx-center)/width)**2)
return noise

Local polish algorithm more concrete:
```
def polish_exact(p, n, budget, rng, best_score):
    t_end=time.monotonic()+budget
    p=np.clip(p,1e-20,None).copy(); p/=p.sum()
    p,best_score=_exact_improve(p,best_score); if None: best_score=evaluate; p
    it=0; stall=0
    sigma=0.08
    while time<t_end:
        # choose block random
        p2=p.copy()
        nblocks=rng.integers(1,4)
        for _ in range(nblocks):
            w=int(rng.integers(max(2, n//200), max(3, n//8)))
            st=int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n-w)))
            amp=float(rng.normal(0.0, sigma))
            # apply smooth within block? Multiplicative block constant enough
            p2[st:st+w] *= float(np.exp(amp))
        if rng.random() < 0.4:
            noise=_smooth_noise(n, rng, sigma*0.5)
            p2 *= np.exp(noise)
        p2 = np.clip(p2,1e-20,None); p2/=p2.sum()
        val=evaluate_sequence(p2.tolist())
        if finite and val < best_score - 1e-10:
            p=p2.copy(); best_score=val; stall=0; sigma=max(0.02, sigma*0.8)
        else:
            stall +=1
            # occasionally try larger sigma
        if stall > 200:
            p=best_p.copy()
            sigma=rng.uniform(0.1,0.3)
            stall=0
        it+=1
    return p,best_score
```

`_as_prob`: handle scalar? If `a` is list length n. Ensure dtype float. Use `np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)`. If all nan, returns None.
```
def _as_prob(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = arr.copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        arr[~np.isfinite(arr)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(arr, 0.0, out=arr)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    arr /= s
    np.maximum(arr, 1e-20, out=arr)
    arr /= arr.sum()
    return arr
```
Clipping max to 1000? We return p with sum1, max not >1. No need.

`_resample`:
```
def _resample(a,n):
    arr=_as_prob(a)
    if arr is None: return np.ones(n)/n
    if arr.size == n: return arr
    old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,arr.size)
    new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    b=np.interp(new, old, arr)
    b=_as_prob(b)
    return b if b is not None else np.ones(n)/n
```

`_fft_conv`:
```
def _fft_conv(p,L,n):
    m=2*n-1
    pad=np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    pad[:n]=p
    P=np.fft.rfft(pad)
    Q=np.fft.irfft(P*P, n=L)
    q=Q[:m]
    # remove negative roundoff
    np.maximum(q, 0.0, out=q)
    maxq=float(np.max(q)) if q.size else 0.0
    return P,q,maxq
```
If p sum maybe not exactly1, Q scales. But p normalized.

`_weights`:
```
def _weights(q,maxq,beta,top_k=0):
    m=q.size
    if maxq <= 1e-300:
       return np.full(m, 1.0/m)
    temp=maxq/float(max(1.0,beta))
    z=q/temp
    zmax=float(np.max(z))
    z = z - zmax
    # z<=0; clip
    np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0, out=z)
    ez=np.exp(z)
    s=ez
    ssum=float(s.sum())
    if ssum <=0 or not finite: uniform
    else s/=ssum
    if top_k and m>top_k:
       # boost top elements moderately
       idx=np.argpartition(-q, top_k-1)[:top_k]  # qsorted? Use np.argpartition(q, m-top_k)[-top_k:]
       s[idx] += 3.0
       s/=s.sum()
    return s
```
Using `np.argpartition(q, m-top_k)[-top_k:]` works if m-top_k >=1. If top_k>=m, all.

`_grad`:
```
def _grad(s,P,L,n):
    m=2*n-1
    sp=np.zeros(L,dtype=np.float64)
    sp[:m]=s
    S=np.fft.rfft(sp)
    g=np.fft.irfft(S*np.conjugate(P), n=L)[:n]
    g *= 2.0
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    return g
```
`np.where` allocate; fine.

`_mul_update`:
```
def _mul_update(p,g,lr,dmax=0.25):
    sg=float(g.sum())
    D=p*sg - g
    D -= D.mean()
    scale=float(np.mean(np.abs(D)))
    if scale < 1e-15:
       scale=float(np.max(np.abs(D)))
    if scale < 1e-15:
       return p
    delta=lr*D/scale
    np.clip(delta, -dmax, dmax, out=delta)
    p = p * np.exp(delta)
    np.maximum(p, 1e-300, out=p)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return p
    p/=s
    return p
```
Could dmax 0.3.

`_smooth_noise`:
```
def _smooth_noise(n,rng,amp):
    noise=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
    idx=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    nterm=int(rng.integers(1,4))
    for _ in range(nterm):
        c=float(rng.uniform(0.0,n))
        w=float(rng.uniform(max(2.0,n/80.0), max(5.0,n/10.0)))
        a=float(rng.normal(0.0, max(1e-9,amp)))
        noise += a*np.exp(-0.5*((idx-c)/w)**2)
    return noise
```
Alloc idx each call; local polish many calls but n small. Could optimize by precomputing? No closures.

`_exact_improve`:
```
def _exact_improve(p,best_score):
    try:
       val=float(evaluate_sequence(p.tolist()))
    except Exception:
       return None,best_score
    if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_score:
       return p.copy(), val
    return None,best_score
```
If p has numpy min 1e-300 tolist okay.

`_score_pool`:
```
def _score_pool(pool,n,L):
    c=[]
    for arr in pool:
       p=_as_prob(arr)
       if p is None: continue
       if p.size != n:
          p=_resample(p,n)
       P,q,mx=_fft_conv(p,L,n)
       score=float(2.0*n*mx)
       if np.isfinite(score):
          c.append((score,len(c),p.copy()))
    c.sort()
    return c
```
If input arr length not n, resample. Good.

def _generate_inits(n,rng):
    inits=[]
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    inits.append(np.ones(n))
    # two half
    p=np.empty(n); h=n//2; p[:h]=4.0/3.0; p[h:]=2.0/3.0; inits.append(p); inits.append(p[::-1])
    # 3 blocks
    for vals in ((4.0/3.0, 1.0, 2.0/3.0), (2.0, 0.8, 0.4), (1.5, 1.0, 0.5), (1.0, 1.6, 0.8)):
       p=np.empty(n)
       n1=n//3; n2=n//3; p[:n1]=vals[0]; p[n1:n1+n2]=vals[1]; p[n1+n2:]=vals[2]
       inits.append(p); inits.append(p[::-1])
    # exponentials
    for g in (-12.,-6.,-3.,-2.,-1.,1.,2.,3.,6.,12.):
       inits.append(np.exp(g*t) + 1e-3)
    # powers
    for a in (0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0):
       p=(t+1e-3)**a; inits.append(p)
       inits.append((1.0-t+1e-3)**a)
       inits.append(0.1 + p)
    # logistic smooth step
    for c in (0.2,0.35,0.5,0.65,0.8):
       for w in (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2):
          p=1.0 + 3.0/(1.0+np.exp((c - t)/w))
          inits.append(p + 0.05)
          p=1.0 + 3.0/(1.0+np.exp((t - c)/w))
          inits.append(p + 0.05)
    # sine
    for ph in (0.0, 0.7, 1.4, 3.0):
       p=1.0 + 0.9*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*t + ph)
       inits.append(np.maximum(p,0.05))
    # random block arrays
    for B in (3,4,5,6,8):
       m=np.abs(rng.normal(1.0,1.0,size=B)) + 0.1
       m/=m.sum()

`_make_block(n,B)`:
```
def _make_block(n,B):
    B=max(1,int(B))
    lengths=np.full(B, n//B, dtype=int)
    lengths[:n % B] += 1
    starts=np.zeros(B+1,dtype=int)
    for i in range(B): starts[i+1]=starts[i]+lengths[i]
    rep=np.repeat(np.arange(B, dtype=int), lengths)
    inv_len=1.0/lengths.astype(float)
    return lengths,starts,rep,inv_len
```

`_run_block_opt`:
```
def _run_block_opt(p0,n,L,m,budget,B,rng):
    if budget <=0 or B<=0: ...
    t0=time.monotonic(); t_end=t0+max(0,budget)
    lengths,starts,rep,inv_len=_make_block(n,B)
    m0=_as_prob(p0)
    if m0 None: m0=np.ones(n)/n
    block_mass=np.zeros(B)
    # use rep? m0[rep] sum per block
    block_mass = np.bincount(rep, weights=m0, minlength=B)
    block_mass = _as_prob(block_mass)
    if block_mass.size != B or block_mass None:
       block_mass=np.ones(B)/B
    best_m=block_mass.copy(); best_score=inf; best_p=m0.copy()
    it=0; last_improve=t0
    while time.monotonic() < t_end - 1.0:
       now=time.monotonic(); frac=min(1,(now-t0)/budget)
       # ensure m positive
       block_mass=np.maximum(block_mass,1e-20); block_mass/=block_mass.sum()
       p=block_mass[rep]*inv_len[rep]
       p /= p.sum()
       P,q,mx=_fft_conv(p,L,n)
       score=float(2.0*n*mx)
       if score < best_score - 1e-10:
           best_score=score; best_m=block_mass.copy(); best_p=p.copy(); last_improve=now
       beta=8.0 + 250.0*frac
       lr=0.25 - 0.20*frac
       s=_weights(q,mx,beta, top_k=max(4, B//2))
       g=_grad(s,P,L,n)
       # aggregate
       if B == 1:
          grad_m=np.array([g.sum()])
       else:
          block_sums=np.add.reduceat(g, starts[:-1])
          grad_m=block_sums / lengths.astype(float)
       D=block_mass*float(grad_m.sum()) - grad_m
       D -= D.mean()
       scale=float(np.mean(np.abs(D)))
       if scale <1e-15:
          scale=float(np.max(np.abs(D)))
       if scale >1e-15:
          delta=lr*D/scale
          np.clip(delta, -0.35,0.35,out=delta)
          block_mass = block_mass * np.exp(delta)
          block_mass = np.maximum(block_mass,1e-20)
          block_mass /= block_mass.sum()
       if now - last_improve > 15.0 and it > 100:
          # kick m
          noise=_smooth_noise(B, rng, 0.1)
          block_mass = block_mass * np.exp(noise)
          block_mass/=block_mass.sum(); last_improve=now
       it += 1
    # return best_p_full and best_score (self)
    return best_p, float(best_score)
```
`np.bincount(rep, weights=m0, minlength=B)` returns length at least B; if rep dtype int. Good.
`np.add.reduceat` with starts[:-1] length B; if B=1, starts[:-1]=[0], returns [sum]. We handle B=1 but B≥3.
Need ensure `lengths` dtype int in division; convert.

`_run_full_opt`:

```
def _run_full_opt(p0,n,L,m,budget,rng,mode='anneal'):
    t0=time.monotonic(); t_end=t0+max(0,budget)
    p=_as_prob(p0)
    if p is None: p=np.ones(n)/n
    best_p=p.copy(); best_self=np.inf
    best_exact_p=None; best_exact_score=np.inf
    if t_end - t0 > 2.0:
       ep,es=_exact_improve(p,best_exact_score)
       if ep is not None: best_exact_p,best_exact_score=ep,es
    last_exact=t0
    it=0
    last_improve=t0
    while time.monotonic() < t_end - 2.0:
       now=time.monotonic(); frac=min(1.0,(now-t0)/budget)
       if mode == 'sharp': beta=40.0+400.0*frac; lr=0.15-0.12*frac; top_k=8
       elif mode == 'random': beta=25.0; lr=0.20; top_k=0
       else: beta=8.0+350.0*frac; lr=0.25-0.22*frac; top_k=16
       P,q,mx=_fft_conv(p,L,n)
       score=float(2.0*n*mx)
       if score < best_self - 1e-10:
           best_self=score; best_p=p.copy(); last_improve=now
       # exact if worthwhile
       if t_end - now > 2.0 and (now - last_exact > 25.0 or it % 20000 == 0):
           ep,es=_exact_improve(p,best_exact_score)
           if ep is not None:
               best_exact_p,best_exact_score=ep,es
           last_exact=now
       s=_weights(q,mx,beta,top_k=top_k)
       g=_grad(s,P,L,n)
       p=_mul_update(p,g,lr,dmax=0.25)
       if now - last_improve > 18.0 and it > 200:
           # p = best_p with noise maybe to escape
           if rng.random() < 0.3:
               p = best_p.copy()
           noise=_smooth_noise(n, rng, 0.10 if mode!='random' else 0.20)
           p = p * np.exp(noise)
           p = _as_prob(p)
           if p is None: p=best_p.copy()
           last_improve=now
       it +=1
    # final exact for best_p
    ep,es=_exact_improve(best_p,best_exact_score)
    if ep is not None: best_exact_p,best_exact_score=ep,es
    if best_exact_p is None:
       return best_p, float(best_self), None, float('inf')
    return best_exact_p, float(best_exact_score) ? Need return also self? Maybe return (best_exact_p, best_exact_score, best_p, best_self)
```

`_polish_exact`:
```
def _polish_exact(p,n,budget,rng,best_score):
    t0=time.monotonic(); t_end=t0+max(0,budget)
    p=_as_prob(p)
    if p is None: p=np.ones(n)/n
    best_p=p.copy()
    ep,bs=_exact_improve(p,best_score)
    if ep is not None: best_p=ep; best_score=bs
    else:
       # If evaluate_sequence returned inf? We need a score. Try now; if inf, set 2n*max self? But must return valid p.
       try: val=float(evaluate_sequence(p.tolist()))
       except: val=2.0
       if np.isfinite(val): best_score=val
       else: best_score=2.0*n*float(np.max(_fft_conv(p, _next_pow2(2*n-1), n)[2]))
       best_p=p.copy()
    it=0; stall=0; sigma=0.10
    idx=np.arange(n,dtype=float) maybe precompute? We'll use _smooth_noise.
    while time.monotonic() < t_end - 1.0:
       p2=best_p.copy()
       nb=int(rng.integers(1,4))
       for _ in range(nb):
          w=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//200), max(3,n//8+1)))
          st=int(rng.integers(0,max(1,n-w)))
          amp=float(rng.normal(0.0,sigma))
          p2[st:st+w] *= float(np.exp(amp))
       if rng.random()<0.35:
          p2 *= np.exp(_smooth_noise(n,rng,sigma*0.5))
       p2=_as_prob(p2)
       if p2 is None: continue
       val=float(evaluate_sequence(p2.tolist()))
       if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_score:
          best_p=p2.copy(); best_score=val; stall=0; sigma=max(0.02,sigma*0.85)
       else:
          stall+=1
          if stall % 100 ==0:
             sigma=min(0.35, sigma*1.15)
       if stall > 500:
          p2=best_p.copy(); p2 *= np.exp(_smooth_noise(n,rng,0.25)); p2=_as_prob(p2)
          if p2 is not None: val=...; update maybe
          stall=0
       it+=1
    return best_p, float(best_score)

def propose_candidate(seed=24, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    budget=float(budget_s)
    if budget <= 10:
       # return height or uniform quickly exact
    deadline=t_start + max(0.0,budget-45.0)  # reserve
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # determine n
    n=4096
    height_arr=None
    try:
       height_arr=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
       if height_arr.size >= 1024 and height_arr.size <= 6000:
           n=int(height_arr.size)
    except Exception:
       height_arr=None
    if n < 512: n=2048
    L=_next_pow2(2*n-1); m=2*n-1
    inits=_generate_inits(n,rng)
    if height_arr is not None and height_arr.size>0:
       inits.append(_resample(height_arr,n))
    # maybe if n != 4096, also add resampled uniform? no.
    pool=list(inits)
    cands=_score_pool(pool,n,L)
    if not cands: cands=[(2.0,0,np.ones(n)/n)]
    # exact top 6
    best_p_exact=None; best_score_exact=np.inf
    for score, idx, p in cands[:6]:
        ep,es=_exact_improve(p,best_score_exact)
        if ep is not None: best_p_exact, best_score_exact=ep,es
    # print progress
    print(f"n={n} initial best exact={best_score_exact:.6f} self={cands[0][0]:.6f}")
    # block pretrain
    # top 2 candidates
    use_full_budget_guard=deadline - time.monotonic()
    if use_full_budget_guard > 900:
       for _,_,p in cands[:2]:
          for B in (8,16,32):
             if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1500: break
             bp,bs=_run_block_opt(p,n,L,m,45.0,B,rng)
             pool.append(bp)

          ep,es=_exact_improve(p,best_score_exact); ...
       print(...)
    # full optimize top candidates

    polish_budget = min(300.0, 0.18*rem)
    full_rem = rem - polish_budget - 20.0
    if full_rem > 60:
       fracs=[0.40,0.20,0.16,0.12,0.08,0.04]
       modes=['anneal','sharp','random','anneal','sharp','random']
       top=cands[:len(fracs)]
       for i, (score,idx,p) in enumerate(top):
          alloc=full_rem*fracs[i]
          if alloc < 45 or time.monotonic() > deadline - 120: break
          self_p, self_sc, exact_p, exact_sc = _run_full_opt(p,n,L,m,alloc,rng,modes[i % len(modes)])
          # update exact

              ep,es=_exact_improve(exact_p,best_score_exact)
              if ep is not None: ...
          # add self_p to pool and exact it

          ep,es=_exact_improve(self_p,best_score_exact)
          ...
          print(f"after full {i} best exact...")
    # final polish

        if ep is not None: best_p_exact=ep; best_score_exact=es
        else: best_score_exact=2.0
    rem=time.monotonic() to deadline
    if rem > 60:
       best_p_exact, best_score_exact = _polish_exact(best_p_exact,n,L,rem*0.5? Actually use min(600,rem),rng,best_score_exact)
    # final evaluate top few p
    final_pool=[best_p_exact]
    for _,_,p in cands[:3]: final_pool.append(p)

    # ensure finite

    seq = best_p_exact.tolist()
    # final exact?

       val=evaluate_sequence(seq)
       if finite: best_score_exact=val
    except: pass
    return seq

def propose_candidate(seed=12345, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    import numpy as np, time already top.
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total=max(1.0,float(budget_s))
    deadline=t0 + max(0.0,total - 40.0)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n=4096; height=None
    try: ...
    except: height=None
    if n<512: n=2048
    L=_next_pow2(2*n-1); m=2*n-1

    if height is not None:
        inits.append(_resample(height,n))
    cands=_score_pool(inits,n,L)
    if not cands:
       p=np.ones(n)/n; val=...; return
    best_exact_p=None; best_exact_score=np.inf
    top_exact_count=6 if len(cands)>6 else len(cands)
    for _,_,p in cands[:top_exact_count]: ...
    print(...)
    # Block
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 1200:
       rem_after_block_reserve=1200
       for c,p in cands[:2]:
          for B in (8,16,32):
             alloc=min(55.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - rem_after_block_reserve)
             if alloc < 20: break
             bp,bs=_run_block_opt(p,n,L,m,alloc,B,rng)
             inits.append(bp)
       cands=_score_pool(inits,n,L)
       for _,_,p in cands[:4]: exact...
    # Full
    rem_full = deadline - time.monotonic() - 150.0
    if rem_full > 90:
       polish_reserve = min(300.0, 0.15*max(1.0,(deadline - time.monotonic())))
       rem_full = deadline - time.monotonic() - 150.0 - polish_reserve
       fracs=[0.38,0.20,0.16,0.14,0.08,0.04]
       modes=['anneal','sharp','random','anneal','sharp','random']
       total_frac=sum(fracs)
       for i,(score,_,p) in enumerate(cands[:len(fracs)]):
          if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120: break
          alloc = (rem_full*fracs[i])/total_frac
          avail = deadline - time.monotonic() - 200.0
          alloc = min(alloc, avail)
          if alloc < 40: break
          ps, ss, pe, es = _run_full_opt(p,n,L,m,alloc,rng,modes[i % len(modes)])

             if ep is not None: best_exact_p,best_exact_score=ep,ese
          ep,ese=_exact_improve(ps,best_exact_score)
          if ep is not None: best_exact_p,best_exact_score=ep,ese
          inits.append(ps)

    # final compare top self cands and best
    final_list=[best_exact_p]
    for _,_,p in cands[:3]: final_list.append(p)
    for p in final_list:
       ep,ese=_exact_improve(p,best_exact_score)
       if ep is not None: best_exact_p,best_exact_score=ep,ese
    # ensure valid and final exact

       seq=[float(x) for x in best_exact_p.tolist()]
       val=evaluate_sequence(seq)
       if np.isfinite(val): best_exact_score=float(val)
    except Exception:
       seq=None
    if seq is None or not valid:
       p=_as_prob(np.ones(n)) or uniform
    return [float(x) for x in best_exact_p]

```python
"""
Search for low-peak nonnegative convolution sequences.

The score is scale invariant, so we optimize probabilities p with sum(p)=1
and minimize max_k (p*p)_k using FFT-based soft-max gradient / multiplicative
updates.  The final candidates are polished with exact evaluate_sequence calls.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _as_prob(a):
    """Return a finite nonnegative float64 array of length len(a) summing to 1."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = arr.copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        arr[~np.isfinite(arr)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(arr, 0.0, out=arr)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    arr /= s
    np.maximum(arr, 1e-20, out=arr)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    arr /= s
    return arr


def _resample(a, n):
    """Resample a nonnegative sequence to length n, normalized to sum 1."""
    arr = _as_prob(a)
    if arr is None:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if arr.size == n:
        return arr
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    b = np.interp(new, old, arr)
    b = _as_prob(b)
    if b is None:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return b


def _next_pow2(x):
    p = 1
    x = int(max(1, x))
    while p < x:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _score_value(p, maxq, n):
    """Exact-style score 2n*max(conv)/sum^2, assuming p already nonnegative."""
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * maxq / (s * s))


def _fft_conv(p, L, n):
    """Linear convolution p*p by zero-padded FFT. Returns P, q, maxq."""
    m = 2 * n - 1
    pad = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    pad[:n] = p
    P = np.fft.rfft(pad)
    Q = np.fft.irfft(P * P, n=L)
    q = Q[:m]
    np.maximum(q, 0.0, out=q)
    maxq = float(np.max(q)) if q.size else 0.0
    return P, q, maxq


def _weights(q, maxq, beta, top_k=0):
    """Soft active weights emphasizing large convolution coefficients."""
    m = int(q.size)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if maxq <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)

    temp = maxq / float(max(1.0, beta))
    z = q / temp
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    z = z - zmax
    np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0, out=z)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    zsum = float(ez.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(zsum) or zsum <= 1e-300:
        s = np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        s = ez / zsum

    top_k = int(max(0, top_k))
    if top_k:
        top_k = min(top_k, m)
        if top_k > 0:
            if top_k >= m:
                idx = np.arange(m)
            else:
                idx = np.argpartition(q, m - top_k)[-top_k:]
            s[idx] += 1.0
            ssum = float(s.sum())
            if ssum > 0 and np.isfinite(ssum):
                s /= ssum
    return s


def _gradient(s, P, L, n):
    """Gradient wrt p of sum_k s_k (p*p)_k."""
    m = 2 * n - 1
    sp = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    sp[:m] = s
    S = np.fft.rfft(sp)
    g = np.fft.irfft(S * np.conjugate(P), n=L)[:n]
    g *= 2.0
    bad = ~np.isfinite(g)
    if bad.any():
        g[bad] = 0.0
    return g


def _mul_update(p, g, lr, dmax=0.25):
    """Mirror-descent multiplicative step on the probability simplex."""
    sg = float(g.sum())
    D = p * sg - g
    D -= D.mean()
    scale = float(np.mean(np.abs(D)))
    if scale < 1e-15:
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(D)))
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-15:
        return p
    delta = lr * D / scale
    np.clip(delta, -dmax, dmax, out=delta)
    p = p * np.exp(delta)
    np.maximum(p, 1e-300, out=p)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return p
    p /= s
    return p


def _smooth_noise(n, rng, amp):
    """Smooth random log-perturbation."""
    noise = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 2 or amp <= 0:
        return noise
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    nterm = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
    lo = max(2.0, min(10.0, n / 80.0))
    hi = max(lo + 1.0, n / 10.0)
    for _ in range(nterm):
        c = float(rng.uniform(0.0, n))
        w = float(rng.uniform(lo, hi))
        a = float(rng.normal(0.0, max(1e-9, amp)))
        noise += a * np.exp(-0.5 * ((idx - c) / w) ** 2)
    return noise


def _exact_improve(p, best_score):
    """Evaluate exact score; return improved copy and score."""
    try:
        val = float(evaluate_sequence(p.tolist()))
    except Exception:
        return None, best_score
    if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_score:
        return p.copy(), val
    return None, best_score


def _score_pool(pool, n, L):
    """Score a pool of arrays; return sorted list (score, idx, p)."""
    out = []
    for arr in pool:
        p = _as_prob(arr)
        if p is None:
            continue
        if p.size != n:
            p = _resample(p, n)
        _, _, mx = _fft_conv(p, L, n)
        score = _score_value(p, mx, n)
        if np.isfinite(score):
            out.append((float(score), len(out), p.copy()))
    out.sort()
    return out


def _generate_inits(n, rng):
    """Generate many structured random initial probability vectors."""
    inits = []
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    # Two-level step, both orientations.
    p = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    h = n // 2
    p[:h] = 4.0 / 3.0
    p[h:] = 2.0 / 3.0
    inits.append(p)
    inits.append(p[::-1])

    # Three-level steps.
    for vals in ((4.0 / 3.0, 1.0, 2.0 / 3.0),
                 (2.0, 0.8, 0.4),
                 (1.5, 1.0, 0.5),
                 (0.8, 1.5, 1.1)):
        p = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        n1 = n // 3
        n2 = n // 3
        p[:n1] = vals[0]
        p[n1:n1 + n2] = vals[1]
        p[n1 + n2:] = vals[2]
        inits.append(p)
        inits.append(p[::-1])

    # Monotone exponentials / powers.
    for g in (-12.0, -6.0, -3.0, -2.0, -1.0, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 6.0, 12.0):
        inits.append(np.exp(g * t) + 1e-3)

    for a in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        p = (t + 1e-3) ** a
        inits.append(p)
        inits.append((1.0 - t + 1e-3) ** a)
        inits.append(0.1 + p)
        inits.append(1.0 / (1.0 + a * t + 1e-3))
        inits.append(1.0 / (1.0 + a * (1.0 - t) + 1e-3))

    # Smooth asymmetric step functions.
    for c in (0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.65, 0.8):
        for w in (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2):
            p = 1.0 + 3.0 / (1.0 + np.exp((c - t) / w))
            inits.append(p + 0.05)
            p = 1.0 + 3.0 / (1.0 + np.exp((t - c) / w))
            inits.append(p + 0.05)

    # Sinusoidal asymmetry.
    for ph in (0.0, 0.7, 1.4, 3.0):
        p = 1.0 + 0.9 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t + ph)
        inits.append(np.maximum(p, 0.05))

    # Random block constants.
    for B in (3, 4, 5, 6, 8):
        m = np.abs(rng.normal(1.0, 1.0, size=B)) + 0.1
        m /= m.sum()
        lengths = np.full(B, n // B, dtype=int)
        lengths[:n % B] += 1
        inits.append(np.repeat(m / lengths.astype(float), lengths))

    # Random smooth Gaussian mixtures.
    for _ in range(16):
        p = np.full(n, 0.1, dtype=np.float64)
        for _ in range(2):
            center = float(rng.uniform(0.0, n))
            w = float(rng.uniform(max(2.0, n / 50.0), max(5.0, n / 8.0)))
            a = float(abs(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)) * 2.0)
            p += a * np.exp(-0.5 * ((idx - center) / w) ** 2)
        inits.append(p)

    return inits


def _make_block(n, B):
    B = max(1, int(B))
    lengths = np.full(B, n // B, dtype=int)
    lengths[:n % B] += 1
    starts = np.zeros(B + 1, dtype=int)
    for i in range(B):
        starts[i + 1] = starts[i] + int(lengths[i])
    rep = np.repeat(np.arange(B, dtype=int), lengths)
    inv_len = 1.0 / lengths.astype(float)
    return lengths, starts, rep, inv_len


def _run_block_opt(p0, n, L, m, budget, B, rng):
    """Optimize piecewise-constant block masses, then lift to full p."""
    if budget <= 0.0:
        p = _as_prob(p0)
        if p is None:
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        _, _, mx = _fft_conv(p, L, n)
        return p, _score_value(p, mx, n)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    tend = t0 + max(0.0, budget)

    lengths, starts, rep, inv_len = _make_block(n, B)
    p0p = _as_prob(p0)
    if p0p is None:
        p0p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    m0 = np.bincount(rep, weights=p0p, minlength=B)
    m0 = _as_prob(m0)
    if m0 is None or m0.size != B:
        m0 = np.ones(B, dtype=np.float64) / float(B)

    block = m0.copy()
    best_block = block.copy()
    best_p = p0p.copy()
    best_score = float("inf")
    last_improve = t0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < tend - 1.0:
        now = time.monotonic()
        frac = min(1.0, (now - t0) / max(1e-12, budget))

        np.maximum(block, 1e-20, out=block)
        block /= block.sum()

        p = block[rep] * inv_len[rep]
        p /= p.sum()
        P, q, mx = _fft_conv(p, L, n)
        score = _score_value(p, mx, n)
        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_block = block.copy()
            best_p = p.copy()
            last_improve = now

        beta = 8.0 + 260.0 * frac
        lr = 0.25 - 0.20 * frac
        s = _weights(q, mx, beta, top_k=max(4, B // 2))
        g = _gradient(s, P, L, n)

        block_sums = np.add.reduceat(g, starts[:-1])
        grad_m = block_sums / inv_len
        D = block * float(grad_m.sum()) - grad_m
        D -= D.mean()
        scale = float(np.mean(np.abs(D)))
        if scale < 1e-15:
            scale = float(np.max(np.abs(D)))
        if scale > 1e-15:
            delta = lr * D / scale
            np.clip(delta, -0.35, 0.35, out=delta)
            block = block * np.exp(delta)
            np.maximum(block, 1e-20, out=block)
            block /= block.sum()

        if now - last_improve > 15.0 and it > 50:
            block = block * np.exp(_smooth_noise(B, rng, 0.10))
            block = _as_prob(block)
            if block is None:
                block = best_block.copy()
            last_improve = now

        it += 1

    return best_p, float(best_score)


def _run_full_opt(p0, n, L, m, budget, rng, mode="anneal"):
    """Full-length multiplicative soft-max optimization."""
    p = _as_prob(p0)
    if p is None:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_self = float("inf")
    best_exact_p = None
    best_exact_score = float("inf")

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    tend = t0 + max(0.0, budget)

    if budget > 5.0:
        ep, es = _exact_improve(p, best_exact_score)
        if ep is not None:
            best_exact_p = ep
            best_exact_score = es

    last_exact = t0
    last_improve = t0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < tend - 2.0:
        now = time.monotonic()
        frac = min(1.0, (now - t0) / max(1e-12, budget))

        if mode == "sharp":
            beta = 40.0 + 420.0 * frac
            lr = 0.15 - 0.12 * frac
            top_k = 64
        elif mode == "random":
            beta = 25.0
            lr = 0.20
            top_k = 0
        else:
            beta = 8.0 + 380.0 * frac
            lr = 0.25 - 0.22 * frac
            top_k = 64

        P, q, mx = _fft_conv(p, L, n)
        score = _score_value(p, mx, n)
        if score < best_self:
            best_self = score
            best_p = p.copy()
            last_improve = now

        if (tend - now) > 2.0 and (now - last_exact > 60.0 or it % 20000 == 0):
            ep, es = _exact_improve(p, best_exact_score)
            if ep is not None:
                best_exact_p = ep
                best_exact_score = es
            last_exact = now

        s = _weights(q, mx, beta, top_k=top_k)
        g = _gradient(s, P, L, n)
        p = _mul_update(p, g, lr, dmax=0.25)

        if now - last_improve > 18.0 and it > 100:
            if rng.random() < 0.3:
                p = best_p.copy()
            kick = 0.10 if mode != "random" else 0.20
            p = p * np.exp(_smooth_noise(n, rng, kick))
            p = _as_prob(p)
            if p is None:
                p = best_p.copy()
            last_improve = now

        it += 1

    for cand in (best_p, p):
        ep, es = _exact_improve(cand, best_exact_score)
        if ep is not None:
            best_exact_p = ep
            best_exact_score = es

    if best_self == float("inf"):
        _, _, mx = _fft_conv(best_p, L, n)
        best_self = _score_value(best_p, mx, n)

    return best_p, float(best_self), best_exact_p, float(best_exact_score)


def _polish_exact(p0, n, L, budget, rng, best_score):
    """Exact-evaluation multiplicative local polish."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    tend = t0 + max(0.0, budget)

    p = _as_prob(p0)
    if p is None:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    best_p = p.copy()
    ep, es = _exact_improve(p, best_score)
    if ep is not None:
        best_p = ep
        best_score = es
    else:
        try:
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(p.tolist()))
            if np.isfinite(val):
                best_score = val
        except Exception:
            _, _, mx = _fft_conv(p, L, n)
            best_score = _score_value(p, mx, n)
        best_p = p.copy()

    sigma = 0.10
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < tend - 1.0:
        p2 = best_p.copy()
        nb = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
        for _ in range(nb):
            w = int(rng.integers(max(2, n // 400), max(3, n // 8 + 1)))
            st = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w)))
            amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, sigma))
            p2[st:st + w] *= float(np.exp(amp))

        if rng.random() < 0.4:
            p2 *= np.exp(_smooth_noise(n, rng, sigma * 0.5))

        p2 = _as_prob(p2)
        if p2 is None:
            continue

        _, _, mx = _fft_conv(p2, L, n)
        self_score = _score_value(p2, mx, n)

        if self_score < best_score + 0.02 or it % 50 == 0:
            ep, es = _exact_improve(p2, best_score)
            if ep is not None:
                best_p = ep
                best_score = es
                stall = 0
                sigma = max(0.02, sigma * 0.85)
            else:
                stall += 1
                if stall % 80 == 0:
                    sigma = min(0.35, sigma * 1.15)
        else:
            stall += 1

        if stall > 400:
            p2 = best_p.copy()
            p2 *= np.exp(_smooth_noise(n, rng, 0.25))
            p2 = _as_prob(p2)
            if p2 is not None:
                ep, es = _exact_improve(p2, best_score)
                if ep is not None:
                    best_p = ep
                    best_score = es
            stall = 0

        it += 1

    return best_p, float(best_score)


def propose_candidate(seed=12345, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Main search entry point."""
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t_start + max(0.0, total - 45.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    # Choose a length close to the available construction, but bounded.
    n = 4096
    height_arr = None
    try:
        hraw = height_sequence_1
        hraw = np.asarray(hraw, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if hraw.size >= 512 and hraw.size <= 6000:
            n = int(hraw.size)
        height_arr = hraw
    except Exception:
        height_arr = None

    if n < 512:
        n = 2048
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    m = 2 * n - 1

    inits = _generate_inits(n, rng)
    if height_arr is not None and height_arr.size > 0:
        inits.append(_resample(height_arr, n))

    cands = _score_pool(inits, n, L)
    if not cands:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        ep, es = _exact_improve(p, float("inf"))
        if ep is not None:
            p = ep
        return [float(x) for x in p]

    best_exact_p = None
    best_exact_score = float("inf")

    # Validate a few best self-scored inits exactly.
    for _, _, p in cands[:6]:
        ep, es = _exact_improve(p, best_exact_score)
        if ep is not None:
            best_exact_p = ep
            best_exact_score = es

    print(f"n={n} initial self={cands[0][0]:.8f} exact={best_exact_score:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Block pre-training: coarse structure improvement.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 1300.0:
        reserve_after_block = 1200.0
        for _, _, p in cands[:2]:
            for B in (8, 16, 32):
                alloc = min(55.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - reserve_after_block)
                if alloc < 20.0:
                    break
                bp, bs = _run_block_opt(p, n, L, m, alloc, B, rng)
                if np.isfinite(bs):
                    inits.append(bp)
        cands = _score_pool(inits, n, L)
        for _, _, p in cands[:5]:
            ep, es = _exact_improve(p, best_exact_score)
            if ep is not None:
                best_exact_p = ep
                best_exact_score = es
        print(f"after block self={cands[0][0]:.8f} exact={best_exact_score:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Full-length optimization on several leading candidates.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    polish_reserve = min(300.0, 0.16 * max(60.0, rem))
    full_rem = rem - 160.0 - polish_reserve
    if full_rem > 100.0:
        fracs = [0.38, 0.20, 0.16, 0.14, 0.08, 0.04]
        modes = ["anneal", "sharp", "random", "anneal", "sharp", "random"]
        total_frac = sum(fracs)

        for i, (_, _, p) in enumerate(cands[:len(fracs)]):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 130.0:
                break
            alloc = min((full_rem * fracs[i]) / total_frac,
                        deadline - time.monotonic() - 180.0)
            if alloc < 40.0:
                break
            self_p, self_score, exact_p, exact_score = _run_full_opt(
                p, n, L, m, alloc, rng, modes[i % len(modes)]
            )

            if exact_p is not None:
                ep, es = _exact_improve(exact_p, best_exact_score)
                if ep is not None:
                    best_exact_p = ep
                    best_exact_score = es

            ep, es = _exact_improve(self_p, best_exact_score)
            if ep is not None:
                best_exact_p = ep
                best_exact_score = es

            inits.append(self_p)
            print(
                f"full[{i}] self={self_score:.8f} exact={best_exact_score:.8f}",
                flush=True
            )

    # Final exact local polish.
    if best_exact_p is None:
        best_exact_p = cands[0][2].copy()
        ep, es = _exact_improve(best_exact_p, best_exact_score)
        if ep is not None:
            best_exact_p = ep
            best_exact_score = es
        else:
            best_exact_score = 2.0

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 70.0:
        alloc = min(600.0, rem - 30.0)
        if alloc > 30.0:
            best_exact_p, best_exact_score = _polish_exact(
                best_exact_p, n, L, alloc, rng, best_exact_score
            )
            print(f"after polish exact={best_exact_score:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Re-score final pool and exact-compare top self-scored full outputs.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 20.0:
        cands = _score_pool(inits, n, L)
        for _, _, p in cands[:4]:
            ep, es = _exact_improve(p, best_exact_score)
            if ep is not None:
                best_exact_p = ep
                best_exact_score = es

    # Ensure the final answer is a valid list.
    final_p = best_exact_p
    if final_p is None:
        final_p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    final_p = _as_prob(final_p)
    if final_p is None:
        final_p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    try:
        final_list = [float(x) for x in final_p.tolist()]
        val = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
        if np.isfinite(val):
            best_exact_score = val
    except Exception:
        final_list = [float(x) for x in final_p]

    return final_list
```
